# Results index

This file inventories 2493 packaged result, trace, and diagnostic artifacts. It describes file formats and paper mapping only; it does not reinterpret or recompute results. The [historical crosswalk](archive/HISTORICAL_FILE_CROSSWALK.csv) gives source and released SHA-256 for copied files.
